correct TeddyUnlike the other Quote Quiz this one is just for quotes from movies and video games, I will give you a quote and you have to tell me which movie/video game it is from (I will not say if it is a movie or game). Each quote will be worth only 1 point unless you give the year it was released and/or who said it, then you get an extra point for each, and if the quote isn't answered within 1 day it gets an extra point for each day it is not answered, so a quote not answered for 1 year will be worth 365 points (though I doubt that will happen). There will also be GP prizes for each question but the amount is at my own discretion and will only be revealed after the question/image is answered. GPs will be awarded ASAP when possible.As most of you do it I am also allowing you to cheat... Google to try find the answer etc. ... just please don't copy and paste it just put the answer while obeying the Forum Rules.Rules:--If you can improve the previous answer (add the year it was released or who said it) then post it and you might receive a point/s also.-If no one has the correct answer within about 24 hours of the question being posted I will add extra point/day and give a hint per day.-Follow the non-spam posting rules.-There will also be GP prizes for each question but the amount is at my own discretion usually 50 GPs.-If you have scored before and have changed your Display name please inform so in a post when you guess another question.-To avoid some easy Google'ing I may spell a few thing a bit different, or spell it different to bypass DGEmu's filter laugh out loud-Some movies and games will be repeated as many of them have several good damn quotes.Scoreboard:tedsb16 - Each quote will be worth only 1 point unless you give the year it was released and/or who said it, then you get an extra point for each, and if the quote isn't answered within 1 day it gets an extra point for each day it is not answered, so a quote not answered for 1 year will be worth 365 points (though I doubt that will happen).
